# LDO-QUIET-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# LDO-QUIET-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.2 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 1e-08 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

PSRR, bidirectional line excursion/recovery and output noise are scored in every
public and independent group. Each nominal group contains 11 native processes:
three DC, three load profiles, two PSRR, two noise and one line profile.
There are 198 public plus 33 independent cases, with no duplicate nominal
characterization stage. Standalone noise_low/noise_high/line/PSRR cost one each.
Line uses the tighter line excursion limit; its initial and recovery bands are
the fixed DC/recovery bands. Repeated endpoint trajectories across starting-VIN
labels are repetitions, not extra coverage.

Output noise integrates native output voltage ASD squared from 10 Hz to 100 kHz
at 50 points/decade. Use a linear-frequency trapezoidal PSD integral and then its
square root. VREF and load are ideal noiseless sources. Keep the spectrum in
V/sqrt(Hz), integrated output in V RMS, and a valid regulated OP. Negative ASD,
missing band endpoints, sparse/nonfinite data and nonregulating OP cannot pass.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.
# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:


n tt bins 180 W min/max 0.36 100.0 L min/max 0.15 100.0
n ss bins 180 W min/max 0.36 100.0 L min/max 0.15 100.0
n ff bins 180 W min/max 0.36 100.0 L min/max 0.15 100.0
p tt bins 108 W min/max 0.42 100.0 L min/max 0.15 100.0
p ss bins 108 W min/max 0.42 100.0 L min/max 0.15 100.0
p ff bins 108 W min/max 0.42 100.0 L min/max 0.15 100.0

MODEL

The key constraints are the 0.2 V minimum headroom, 100 µA light-load overhead, and stability with both output capacitors. I’m starting with a PMOS pass device, a large PMOS input pair for low noise, and a replica-biased gate driver whose current increases with load.

0:03:59

/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'SPICE'
* PMOS LDO with replica-biased gate drive
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFB1 VOUT FB 100k
RFB2 FB VSS 100k
CFB VOUT FB 10p
* Resistive bias and PMOS tail mirror
RB PB VSS 150k
XPB PB PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=10 l=2 nf=1 m=1
XPT TS PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=40 l=2 nf=1 m=1
* Error amplifier, falling output for rising feedback
XPI1 ND VREF TS VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=1 nf=1 m=8
XPI2 EA FB TS VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=1 nf=1 m=8
XNL1 ND ND VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=2 nf=1 m=2
XNL2 EA ND VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=2 nf=1 m=2
* Gate buffer with a scaled replica of the pass transistor
XDR PG EA VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=30 l=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XREP PG PG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XPASS VOUT PG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 nf=1 m=24
* Error amplifier frequency shaping
RCOMP EA CZ 100k
CCOMP CZ VSS 70p
.ends DUT

/bin/bash -lc "cat > summarize.py <<'PY'
import sys,json
for name in sys.argv[1:]:
 x=json.load(open(name)); print(name, 'ok',x.get('ok'), 'revision',x.get('revision'), 'remaining', x.get('remaining_seconds'))
 if not x.get('cases'): print(json.dumps(x,indent=2)); continue
 for c in x['cases']:
  m=c.get('measurements',{}); row={'case':c['case_id'],'status':c.get('status')}
  for k in ('vout_v','overhead_a','psrr_db','op_regulating'): 
   if k in m: row[k]=m[k]
  if 'noise' in m: row['noise_uv']=m['noise'].get('output_noise_rms_v',0)*1e6
  if 'envelope' in m: row['envelope']=m['envelope']; row['recoveries']=[a.get('recovery_s') for a in m.get('recovery_windows',[])]
  if c.get('solver_errors'):row['errors']=c['solver_errors']
  print(json.dumps(row))
 s=x.get('summary',{})
 print('Summary:', json.dumps({k:v for k,v in s.items() if k"'!='"'params'}))
 print('Other:', json.dumps({k:v for k,v in x.items() if k not in ['summary','cases']}))
PY
python3 summarize.py m001.json"

MODEL

The resistive load reduced ringing and noise, but it weakened DC regulation. I’m preserving high DC gain and applying that damping through a capacitor. I’ve also lowered the comparison voltage to 0.4 V to give the cold amplifier more headroom.

MODEL

The reference divider is consuming much of the capacitance budget needed for compensation. I’m testing matched transistor level shifters on the reference and feedback inputs. They should provide the needed headroom while drawing essentially no reference DC current, leaving more capacitance available for damping.

import json,subprocess,sys
start=int(sys.argv[1]); analysis=sys.argv[2]; cl=float(sys.argv[3])
for k,pvt in enumerate(sys.argv[4:]):
 id=f'm{start+k:03d}'
 request={'action':'measure','experiment_id':id,'analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt,'cl':cl}
 with open('REQUEST.json','w') as f:json.dump(request,f)
 with open(id+'.json','w') as f:subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],stdout=f,check=True)
 x=json.load(open(id+'.json'))
 print(id,pvt,cl,'ok',x.get('ok'),flush=True)
 for c in x.get('cases',[]):
  m=c.get('measurements',{})
  print(c['case_id'], 'vout',m.get('vout_v'),'overhead',m.get('overhead_a'),'noise_uv',m.get('noise',{}).get('output_noise_rms_v',0)*1e6,flush=True)
 print('misses',[(r['case_id'],r['metric'],r['measured']) for r in x.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]) if r['verdict']"'!='"'PASS'],flush=True)
 print('remaining',x.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'),flush=True)

import json,subprocess
pvts=['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
plan=[]
for index,(cl,pvt) in enumerate((cl,pvt) for cl in [1e-7,1e-6] for pvt in pvts):
 id=f'm{48+index:03d}'
 req={'action':'measure','experiment_id':id,'analysis':'nominal','pvt':pvt,'cl':cl}
 plan.append(req)
 with open(id+'-request.json','w') as f:json.dump(req,f)
with open('public_grid_plan.json','w') as f:json.dump(plan,f,indent=2)
for req in plan:
 id=req['experiment_id']
 with open(id+'.json','w') as f:subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request',id+'-request.json'],stdout=f,check=True)
 x=json.load(open(id+'.json')); rows=x.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[])
 misses=[(r['case_id'],r['metric'],r['measured'],r['verdict']) for r in rows if r['verdict']"'!='"'PASS']
 print(id, req['pvt'],req['cl'],x.get('revision'),'ok',x.get('ok'),'cases',len(x.get('cases',[])), 'misses',misses,'remaining',x.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'),flush=True)
 if not x.get('ok'): print(json.dumps(x),flush=True)

/bin/bash -lc "cat > evaluate_grid.py <<'PY'
import json,os
plan=json.load(open('public_grid_plan.json'))
completed=[]; misses=[]; metrics={}; revisions=set(); case_count=0; kcl=0.0; resources=None
for req in plan:
 path=req['experiment_id']+'.json'
 try:x=json.load(open(path))
 except (FileNotFoundError,json.JSONDecodeError):continue
 completed.append(req); revisions.add(x.get('revision')); case_count+=len(x.get('cases',[]))
 if resources is None: resources=x.get('summary',{}).get('params',{}).get('resources')
 if not x.get('ok'):misses.append({'request':req,'error':x})
 for c in x.get('cases',[]):
  if c.get('status')"'!='"'OK':misses.append({'request':req,'case':c['case_id'],'status':c.get('status')})
  kcl=max(kcl,abs(c.get('measurements',{}).get('kcl_residual_a',0)))
 for row in x.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]):
  if row['verdict']"'!='"'PASS':misses.append({'request':req,**row})
  value=row.get('measured')
  if value is None:continue
  key=row['metric']
  if key.startswith('recovery_'):key='recovery_s'
  if key in ['envelope_error_v','recovery_s']:key=('line_' if row['case_id']=='line' else 'load_')+key
  if key=='overhead_a':key='light_overhead_a' if row['limit']==0.0001 else 'other_overhead_a'
  old=metrics.get(key)
  if old is None or (value>old['measured'] if row['direction']=='<=' else value<old['measured']):metrics[key]={'measured':value,'limit':row['limit'],'pvt':req['pvt'],'cl':req['cl'],'case':row['case_id']}
report={'groups_complete':len(completed),'groups_required':len(plan),'native_cases_complete':case_count,'revisions':sorted(revisions),'misses':misses,'worst_metrics':metrics,'maximum_kcl_residual_a':kcl,'resources':resources}
json.dump(report,open('public_grid_summary.json','w'),indent=2)
print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))
PY"

import json,hashlib
report=json.load(open('public_grid_summary.json'))
assert report['groups_complete']==18 and report['native_cases_complete']==198 and not report['misses']
assert open('dut.spice','rb').read()==open('candidate_fullgrid.spice','rb').read()
print('DUT SHA256',hashlib.sha256(open('dut.spice','rb').read()).hexdigest())
rationale='Continuous-time PMOS LDO with a 2:1 feedback divider. Matched, large NMOS input source followers lower the amplifier common mode across temperature and process without drawing DC current from VREF. A PMOS differential error amplifier, long-channel NMOS mirror load, and resistor-biased PMOS tail provide gain with low noise. A 1:24 PMOS pass-device replica biases the NMOS gate driver so drive current follows load. The 50 pF series-R shunt compensation, 47 pF feedback feedforward capacitor, and 3 pF amplifier capacitor stabilize both bench capacitor values and suppress high-frequency supply feedthrough. The fixed netlist uses only approved MOS devices and positive resistors/capacitors, with MOS W*L of 8965 square micrometres and 100 pF added capacitance. All 198 public native cases pass: maximum DC error 2.566 mV, load excursion 19.564 mV, load recovery 13.983 us, light-load overhead 93.695 uA, output noise 29.266 uV RMS; minimum PSRR 47.100 dB at 1 kHz and 35.273 dB at 1 MHz. Worst line excursion is 4.443 mV and VREF DC current is zero in measured cases.'
open('submission_rationale.txt','w').write(rationale+'\\n')
json.dump({'action':'validate'},open('REQUEST.json','w'))
PY

def compact(v):
 if isinstance(v,dict):
  return {k:compact(a) for k,a in v.items() if k not in ['params','normalized_params','elements','normalized_netlist','netlist','source','dut']}
 if isinstance(v,list):

   counts=collections.Counter(a.get('verdict',a.get('status','unspecified')) for a in v if isinstance(a,dict))

  return [compact(a) for a in v]
 return v
